This is docs/BACKLOG.md BL-65, still open. — how: Build civilware/gnomon, point it at 127.0.0.1:20000, let it index from height 0, then query it for the two NFAs already on the simulator (fa06a61a… DERO-quoted, c1a54ead… wXMR-quoted, both registered under the factory 83ae27eb…). Confirm it reports owner, listType, startPrice, bidCount and version 1.1.1 for the first, and either indexes or cleanly ignores the second — a wXMR price reported as DERO would be the failure to look for. Record the output under docs/.
- **[Founder NFTs, user NFAs, the NFA standard (founders.bas, nfa.bas, nft.bas, factory NFA registration, and the app's asset screens) — §9–§12] A rendered UI pass showing a hostile NFA: install an NFA whose nameHdr is "wXMR", whose collection duplicates a real one, and whose fileURL is a userinfo URL, then read the NFTs tab and the #nfa/<scid> page as PNGs.** — why: The UI pass needs a wallet to install and register the NFA; my assignment is read-only on the simulator. APP-1 and APP-2 are therefore proven from the code and from Node tests over app/ui.js and app/feed.js, not from a screenshot. — how: With a funded simulator wallet: `cd tools && go run ./simnfa -daemon 127.0.0.1:20000 -wallet 127.0.0.1:300NN` with nameHdr "wXMR", collection set to an existing collection string, and fileURL "https://dmz.tela.official-mint.com@attacker.invalid/claim"; register it with RegisterNFA; then `tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-nfa-hostile` and read the NFTs-tab and NFA-page PNGs with the Read tool. Expect a green "Verified" badge beside the name and the userinfo URL rendered as its own link text.
- **[Founder NFTs, user NFAs, the NFA standard (founders.bas, nfa.bas, nft.bas, factory NFA registration, and the app's asset screens) — §9–§12] Drive the NFA-1 chain end to end on the simulator daemon rather than the in-memory harness — three wallets, a real auction, a real empty BuyItNow, a real empty Bid.** — why: Needs three funded wallets to sign; read-only assignment. The harness runs derohe's own dvm.Execute_sc_function with the real SanityCheckExternalTransfers, so the contract behaviour is the chain's; what a simulator run would add is confirmation that the gas estimator does not misjudge the height/timestamp-gated calls (the estimator runs at height 0, as tools/simswap works around). — how: Install nfa.bas from reference.Canon with wallet A; Start an auction of 1 hour; Bid from wallet B; wait past endBlockTime (the simulator advances one block per ~18 s, or mine forward); call BuyItNow with no transfers from wallet C at a fixed fee (the estimator will refuse it); read DERO.GetSC for currBidAmt/currBidAddr; Start a second listing from A; let it end; call Bid with no transfers from C at a fixed fee; confirm the unit reaches B.
- **[Founder NFTs, user NFAs, the NFA standard (founders.bas, nfa.bas, nft.bas, factory NFA registration, and the app's asset screens) — §9–§12] Install and exercise the Founders Pass at the mainnet shape the directive names — maxsupply 20, reserve <= 4 — on the simulator, with the treasury bot running.** — why: tools/simdeploy defaults to -pass-max 500 -pass-reserve 50 and the deployed simulator pass aee67463… is a 500-edition legacy build that predates the minhst key entirely. Installing a 20-edition pass needs a funded deployer wallet. — how: `cd tools && go run ./simdeploy -network simulator -daemon 127.0.0.1:20000 -w0 <deployer rpc> -w2 <treasury rpc> -pass-max 20 -pass-reserve 4 -pass-price 500000 -pass-share 5000 -pass-mfee 250`, then run tools/treasurybot against it before any wallet registers, then Register one pass from a second wallet and Claim. Compare the DERO received against `dep`.
- **[Monero side and the hot-wallet question (directive §2, §4-XMR, §20, §22): tools/xmrswap/monero.go + fakewallet.go + swap.go's Monero paths, tools/vaultsigner's (unbuilt) Monero backend, and the XMR deposit/withdrawal test matrix] A Monero stagenet deposit and withdrawal through the xmrswap companion — the whole happy path, the refund path and the punish path against a real monero-wallet-rpc. This is the test that would have caught XMR-2 and the --wallet-dir half of XMR-4 by itself.** — why: There is no monerod or monero-wallet-rpc on this machine and I may not stand one up against a public network from here. tools/simhtlc refuses to run without -fake-monero (tools/simhtlc/main.go:69-71): there is no other Monero backend in the tree. — how: On each of two machines (or two data dirs): `monerod --stagenet --prune-blockchain` and `monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet --daemon-address 127.0.0.1:38081 --rpc-bind-port 38082 --wallet-dir ./xmrwallets --disable-rpc-login --trusted-daemon`. --wallet-dir is MANDATORY (wallet_rpc_server.cpp:3700-3705 and 3912-3917) and --restricted-rpc must NOT be set (same lines). Fund Alice's stagenet wallet from a faucet. Install htlc.bas on the local DERO simulator (`cd tools && go run ./simhtlc -fake-monero -prover "<cmd>"` prints a scid, or install it by hand). Then, against the simulator daemon so the lowered minimums are accepted: `xmrswap bob -state bob.json -dero 127.0.0.1:30006 -daemon 127.0.0.1:20000 -htlc <scid> -monero http://127.0.0.1:38082 -net stagenet -prover "<cmd>" -channel ./swap -amount-dero 100000 -amount-xmr 1000000000000 -t1-min 10 -t2-min 6 -confs 10 -stable 2` and the matching `xmrswap alice -state alice.json -dero 127.0.0.1:30007 ...`. Record the full get_transfers / get_balance / sweep_all transcripts into tools/xmrswap/testdata. Expected first result, from this audit: Bob reaches sweep_ready and the very next SweepAll answers -37 because on_generate_from_keys never refreshes (wallet_rpc_server.cpp:4085-4097) — that is XMR-2. Repeat with the fix and assert the sweep lands. Then the refund path (kill Bob after his lock, restart him after t1) and the punish path (kill Bob for good), asserting the contract balance is 0 after each and that Alice's recovery sweep also lands.
- **[Monero side and the hot-wallet question (directive §2, §4-XMR, §20, §22): tools/xmrswap/monero.go + fakewallet.go + swap.go's Monero paths, tools/vaultsigner's (unbuilt) Monero backend, and the XMR deposit/withdrawal test matrix] Any deposit or withdrawal through tools/vaultsigner against a real Monero chain — the vault's wrap and unwrap for XMR.** — why: tools/vaultsigner has no Monero backend at all: -backend monero exits at main.go:516 with 'not implemented yet'. Nothing in the tree derives a subaddress, runs a multisig round, builds a transfer or reads a Monero balance for the vault. There is no partial implementation to point at a node. — how: docs/HANDOVER.md §5.3 is the build plan and its steps are the test steps: (1) monerod --stagenet plus one monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet per signer with `set enable-multisig-experimental 1`; (2) the key ceremony prepare_multisig → make_multisig(k) → exchange_multisig_keys until is_multisig.ready, recording the multisig address and `query_key view_key`; (3) implement Address(j) as get_address/create_address on account 0 subaddress j; (4) Deposits() as get_transfers{in:true,pool:false} filtered by subaddr_indices with unlock_time==0 required and Finalized() = tip−10; (5) Reserve() as get_balance minus unconfirmed paying/paid intents; (6) Release(j,dest,amount) with the `dmz:j=<j>` tx-note idempotency lookup, then do_not_relay + multisig_txset + sign_multisig + submit_multisig. Only then: Register/Attest on the simulator vault, send real stagenet XMR to the attested subaddress, watch Mint, then Burn and watch Released. Check on the way out that the reserve minus the Monero network fee still satisfies reserve >= sup − inflight — the fee is unaccounted on chain and this is where that shows up first.
- **[Monero side and the hot-wallet question (directive §2, §4-XMR, §20, §22): tools/xmrswap/monero.go + fakewallet.go + swap.go's Monero paths, tools/vaultsigner's (unbuilt) Monero backend, and the XMR deposit/withdrawal test matrix] Observing whether a real monero-wallet-rpc reports a transfer entry's `unlock_time` as the raw transaction field (what the code assumes) or as 'Number of blocks until transfer is safely spendable' (what the published reference says).** — why: The two readings disagree about the ORDINARY case. If the reference's wording were right, a normal output at 3 confirmations would report unlock_time 7, confirmedAtJoint (swap.go:526-533) would skip it, and Bob's gate would never open at all. I settled it from the monero source (fill_transfer_entry sets entry.unlock_time = pd.m_unlock_time) rather than by observation — so it is decided, but not measured. — how: On the stagenet rig above, send an ordinary transfer with no unlock_time and call get_transfers{in:true} at 3 confirmations: unlock_time must be 0 and locked must be true. Then send one with unlock_time set and confirm the raw value comes back. Record both as fixtures under tools/xmrswap/testdata so fakewallet.go is checked against a real transcript rather than against the reference's prose.
- **[Monero side and the hot-wallet question (directive §2, §4-XMR, §20, §22): tools/xmrswap/monero.go + fakewallet.go + swap.go's Monero paths, tools/vaultsigner's (unbuilt) Monero backend, and the XMR deposit/withdrawal test matrix] A UI pass over app/wrap.js's deposit walkthrough with a wallet connected, to see what a depositor is actually told about confirmations and deposit addresses (XMR-6).** — why: Outside my dimension — the vault reader already established that the app's per-user keys are spelled dero1… while the contract keys by raw bytes, so those rows never render — and I was assigned read-only wallets. — how: tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-xmr, then read the PNGs with the Read tool.
- **[Zcash side (directive §3, §4-ZEC, §21): tools/zecswap (script/sign/sighash/orchard/blake2 + CLI + vectors), contracts/swap.bas and board.bas as the DERO counterpart of the transparent-ZEC market, docs/ZEC_SWAP.md against the current Zcash protocol spec / ZIP 225 / 244 / 317 / 255 / 257 / 258 / 203 / 320 and BIP 65, and the requirements for the not-yet-built watcher and broadcast layer.] The Zcash testnet run: fund the P2SH, claim with the preimage, refund after CLTV, record txids (BL-42).** — why: No Zcash node exists on this machine and the directive forbids touching any chain but the local DERO simulator. tools/zecswap has no node client, no transaction builder and no broadcast, so even with a node the spends would have to be assembled by hand. — how: Run zebrad on testnet with the RPC endpoint enabled (zcashd is end-of-support and will not serve NU6.3). Derive two P2PKH keys. Run `zecswap script -hash <lower-case 64 hex> -claim <tm-addr> -refund <tm-addr> -cltv <tip+96> -net testnet` and send exactly wantAmount to the printed t2 address from any transparent testnet wallet. Read consensus.nextblock from getblockchaininfo and build Tx{BranchID: that value, In: the funding outpoint with Value and ScriptPubKey filled in, Out: P2PKH to the claim key for wantAmount − 10,000 zat, LockTime 0, ExpiryHeight 0}; SigDigest(0, SigHashAll); Key.Sign; ClaimScriptSig; Serialize; sendrawtransaction. Then repeat on a second funding with RefundScriptSig, LockTime = cltv, Sequence 0xfffffffe, ExpiryHeight 0, broadcast at height cltv+1. Record all txids in docs/history/HISTORY.md. Repeat the whole run after the ZEC-1 fix so the shipped script — not the 94-byte one — is the one that was proven.
- **[Zcash side (directive §3, §4-ZEC, §21): tools/zecswap (script/sign/sighash/orchard/blake2 + CLI + vectors), contracts/swap.bas and board.bas as the DERO counterpart of the transparent-ZEC market, docs/ZEC_SWAP.md against the current Zcash protocol spec / ZIP 225 / 244 / 317 / 255 / 257 / 258 / 203 / 320 and BIP 65, and the requirements for the not-yet-built watcher and broadcast layer.] Mempool and relay behaviour of the two spends: whether a redeem script carrying a non-minimal CLTV push (ZEC-6) is actually rejected by a standard node, and whether a conflicting refund can displace a claim already in the mempool (the ZEC-3 race).** — why: Both are node policy, not script semantics; zcash_script evaluates a script in isolation and cannot answer either. No Zcash node here. — how: On testnet, build two otherwise identical HTLCs with cltv = tip+96 and cltv = 5, fund both, and try to broadcast the refund of each after its height; record which sendrawtransaction is refused and with what error. For the race: fund one script, broadcast the claim, and from a second node immediately broadcast a refund spending the same outpoint with a higher fee; record whether either node accepts the replacement and how long the first-seen transaction is held.
- **[Zcash side (directive §3, §4-ZEC, §21): tools/zecswap (script/sign/sighash/orchard/blake2 + CLI + vectors), contracts/swap.bas and board.bas as the DERO counterpart of the transparent-ZEC market, docs/ZEC_SWAP.md against the current Zcash protocol spec / ZIP 225 / 244 / 317 / 255 / 257 / 258 / 203 / 320 and BIP 65, and the requirements for the not-yet-built watcher and broadcast layer.] Measured reorg statistics for Zcash, to replace the argued C_zec = 10 confirmations and the argued cltv = tip+96 window (BL-30, BL-52).** — why: Needs a synced archival Zcash node and a year of block data; neither is available here, and docs/ZEC_SWAP.md §5 is explicit that the numbers are simulator policy until measured. — how: Sync zebrad with archival state, walk the chain recording every side chain the node saw and its depth, and derive the confirmation depth at which the observed reorg probability is below the value at risk of a typical offer. Do the same for DERO to check that STABLE_LIMIT = 8 is the right wait on the other leg, then rewrite the §5 table with measured numbers and mark BL-30/BL-52 done.
- **[Zcash side (directive §3, §4-ZEC, §21): tools/zecswap (script/sign/sighash/orchard/blake2 + CLI + vectors), contracts/swap.bas and board.bas as the DERO counterpart of the transparent-ZEC market, docs/ZEC_SWAP.md against the current Zcash protocol spec / ZIP 225 / 244 / 317 / 255 / 257 / 258 / 203 / 320 and BIP 65, and the requirements for the not-yet-built watcher and broadcast layer.] Whether a light wallet or indexer a real taker uses can see the P2SH funding at all, and whether a maker's wallet adding shielded change to the claim still lets the taker read s.** — why: Needs a testnet node plus a lightwalletd and a real wallet. The Orchard-parsing path is exercised only by the ZIP 244 vectors, and a Sapling change output makes ParseV5 return ErrShielded, so the secret would have to be read from the node's decoded JSON instead — no code does that. — how: After the BL-42 run, claim once from a wallet configured to produce Orchard change and once from one producing Sapling change; in each case have the taker's side try ParseV5 on the raw claim and, on ErrShielded, fall back to getrawtransaction verbose and read the scriptSig's third push. Record which path each wallet takes and add whichever fallback is needed to the watcher.
- **[DERO contracts and DVM semantics — make it lose money (directive §6, §13, §17)] Whether Civilware's hard-coded artificerAddr (dero1qy0khp9s9yw2h0eu20xmy9lth3zp5cacmx3rwt6k45l568d2mmcf6qgcsevzx, nfa.bas:60) is a REGISTERED account on DERO mainnet. This decides whether DVM-1 case (b) is a mainnet-wide defect or only a testnet/private-chain one.** — why: No mainnet daemon is reachable from here and the directive forbids touching mainnet. The local simulator has its own registry, and tests/nfa_test.go:76-80 registers that address by hand for every NFA test, which is exactly why no existing test can see the question. — how: Against any public DERO mainnet node: curl -s -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -X POST http://<mainnet-node>:10102/json_rpc -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"1","method":"DERO.GetEncryptedBalance","params":{"address":"dero1qy0khp9s9yw2h0eu20xmy9lth3zp5cacmx3rwt6k45l568d2mmcf6qgcsevzx","topoheight":-1}}'. A result is a registered account; "account unregistered" or a NOT AVAILABLE status means every NFA sale with artificerFee > 0 on mainnet destroys the seller's remainder and the protocol fee. Repeat for the mainnet treasury address before deployment, and add both to deploy/README.md's mainnet gate.
- **[DERO contracts and DVM semantics — make it lose money (directive §6, §13, §17)] End-to-end reproduction of the half-commit on a running derod (simulator), rather than through tests/harness. The harness runs derohe's own dvm.SanityCheckExternalTransfers and dvm.ProcessExternal (tests/harness/chain.go:185-196), so the panic and the transfer ordering ARE the chain's; what I read rather than executed is the recover in blockchain.process_transaction_sc (transaction_execute.go:277-282) and the unconditional tree commit in blockchain.go:971-1004, 1010-1012.** — why: I was given read-only wallet access on the simulator, so I could not sign an install or a purchase. — how: With a simulator wallet: install contracts/nfa.bas via reference.Canon with protoFee 50 and a registered treasury; Start a sale with charityDonatePerc 20 and a charityDonateAddr generated locally and never funded (any fresh dero1… string from a wallet that has not registered); BuyItNow from a second wallet for 1,000,000. Then DERO.GetSC on the NFA and confirm owner == the buyer, scBalance == 0, previousSalePrice == 1000000 while the seller's balance is unchanged and DERO.GetSC balance for the quote is 0. tools/simnfa/main.go is the closest existing driver to extend.
- **[DERO contracts and DVM semantics — make it lose money (directive §6, §13, §17)] Whether the same half-commit is reachable through the browser app end to end (the Start sheet accepting an unregistered charity, then a buy).** — why: The UI pass is serialised on the same lock and I read app/nfas.js:88-110 and app/forms.js:185-251 instead. The code path is unambiguous — V.canonAddress is a format check only — but I did not drive it. — how: tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-dvm, extended to open #nfa/<scid>, Start a sale with a charity share and a fresh unfunded address, and screenshot the sheet; then read the PNGs.
- **[DERO contracts and DVM semantics — make it lose money (directive §6, §13, §17)] Real Monero stagenet / Zcash testnet legs of anything.** — why: Out of this dimension and impossible here (no Monero backend in tools/vaultsigner, tools/xmrswap has only ever run against its in-process fake, tools/zecswap has no broadcast). — how: docs/HANDOVER.md §5.3/§5.4 and the xmrswap/zecswap subsystem maps carry the exact steps; not my dimension.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Executing the FE-2 registry-poisoning attack on the simulator (RegisterPool with a listed NFA's scid) and observing the app break in the browser** — why: My brief is read-only on the simulator wallets; sending a factory registration needs a funded wallet. The crash itself is reproduced at unit level over the real app modules, but the on-chain half is inference. — how: From any funded simulator wallet (127.0.0.1:30000-30021), wallet RPC `transfer` with scid = 83ae27eba7c8a225398f5fb699da9f71c039b769b0d3b5aabf09e75ce390acd7, sc_rpc = [{entrypoint,S,RegisterPool},{scid,H,c1a54ead...}], transfers = [{destination: <DERO.GetRandomAddress>, amount:0, burn:10000}], ringsize 2. Wait for inclusion, then reload http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html and read the console: expect TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'length') from feed.js decorate and an errbox on Home. IRREVERSIBLE on that chain - the factory has no prune - so use a disposable simulator, not the shared one.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Executing the BC-1 drain against the live vulnerable listed launch 9f5d93affef8217a... (real = 145,336 atomic DERO) to confirm the double payment end to end** — why: Read-only wallet brief. The contract-side proof already exists in the repo (d1c5a89's commit message records 2,052,806 taken from a 2,150,000 pot on the real DVM, and TestNoExternalSendBeforeAFailureExit fails on the pre-fix contract). What I established is the frontend half: that hash is an accepted "Verified" hash today and that market is listed. — how: From a funded wallet, call SwapDeroForAssetTo(dest = your address, min_out = a value above what the curve can pay, publish = 0) on 9f5d93affef8217a... with a DERO burn sized to overshoot the remaining curve, ring 16. Read the wallet balance and the contract's `real` key before and after; repeat until `real` is 0. Disposable simulator only.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Confirming the TELA INDEX becomes immutable (owner == "anon") when installed above ring size 2** — why: tools/telainstall has no flag for it and installing an INDEX needs a wallet. — how: On the simulator, run tela-cli (or a patched telainstall) install-index answering ring size 16, then DERO.GetSC {scid: <new index>, keysstring: ["owner","commit"]} and expect owner == "anon"; then attempt UpdateCode from the installing wallet and expect RETURN 1.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Observing the FE-5 signer disclosure at a node the user does not control** — why: Requires standing a logging proxy in front of the daemon and driving a signed transaction, which needs a wallet. — how: Run a WebSocket logging proxy on 127.0.0.1:20001 forwarding to 20000, set it in Settings, connect a wallet, and start any ring-2 action; the DERO.GetGasEstimate frame logged before the wallet prompt will carry `signer: dero1...` plus the entrypoint and amounts.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Any real TELA install, mainnet publish, or genconfig -dist run** — why: genconfig -dist rewrites app/config.js and the directive reserves that for the final validator; telainstall needs the deployer wallet and would publish to a chain. — how: Leave both to the final validator: `(cd tools && go run ./genconfig -dist -network simulator)` for the bundle, and the deploy/README.md TELA runbook with the deployer wallet for the install.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Cross-origin staging of FE-7 (a hostile TELA app inheriting DMZ's port) and FE-11 (framing)** — why: Both need a second TELA app installed and a controlled port collision, or an attacker-hosted page; neither is available here and neither changes the source-level conclusion. — how: FE-7: serve any page on 127.0.0.1:8082, set localStorage veil.settings from its console, stop it, then serve DMZ on the same port and reload. FE-11: serve a page containing <iframe src="http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html"> and confirm it renders and takes clicks.
- **[TELA, frontend, wallet interaction, supply chain (directive §15, §16, §26, §35)] Real Monero stagenet / Zcash testnet deposit and withdrawal through the Wrap screen, to confirm FE-3's user-visible consequences end to end** — why: tools/vaultsigner has no Monero or Zcash backend (-backend monero|zcash exits at main.go:470-476); only the `sim` JSON-file backend exists, and the operator runs no stagenet nodes. — how: Run monerod --stagenet plus one monero-wallet-rpc per signer, build the vaultsigner Monero backend against them, Register from a wallet on the simulator vault, wait for Attest, pay the attested address, watch Mint, then Burn and observe Released - checking at each step whether the Wrap screen ever shows the index, the deposit address, or the withdrawal row.
- **[DERO contracts — funds stuck forever, and states that need a human (directive §5, §29, LIVENESS BY CONSTRUCTION)] Reproducing LIV-01 through LIV-10 as real transactions on the local DERO simulator (daemon 127.0.0.1:20000, app at http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html)** — why: My wallet assignment for this run was read-only, so I could not sign any simulator transaction. Everything here ran on the in-memory harness, which executes derohe's own dvm.Execute_sc_function, SanityCheckExternalTransfers, ErrorRevert and ProcessExternal — the same code paths a node runs — but does not exercise gas metering, mempool ordering or reorgs. — how: For LIV-01: install nfa.bas from a simulator wallet with artificerFee 5, Start a sale, BuyItNow from a second wallet, then DERO.GetSC the NFA and confirm owner is the buyer and scBalance 0, and confirm via wallet GetBalance that the seller's balance did not increase. For LIV-02: install nfa.bas, Start an auction, bid from wallet A, advance past endBlockTime, call BuyItNow with no value from wallet B, then GetSC and confirm currBidAmt is still set while the unit is back with the seller. For LIV-03/04: tools/simvault -install -deposit -burn against a 2-of-3 simulator vault, then -paying and attempt -reclaim; for LIV-04 pass a refund address generated but never registered.
- **[DERO contracts — funds stuck forever, and states that need a human (directive §5, §29, LIVENESS BY CONSTRUCTION)] Confirming whether the hard-coded artificer address dero1qy0khp9s9yw2h0eu20xmy9lth3zp5cacmx3rwt6k45l568d2mmcf6qgcsevzx is registered on DERO mainnet** — why: No mainnet daemon is reachable here and the directive forbids touching mainnet. This decides whether LIV-01's variant (b) is a mainnet defect or only a simulator/testnet one — the charity variant (a) is live on every network regardless. — how: Against any DERO mainnet daemon: curl -s -X POST http://<mainnet-node>:10102/json_rpc -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"1","method":"DERO.GetEncryptedBalance","params":{"address":"dero1qy0khp9s9yw2h0eu20xmy9lth3zp5cacmx3rwt6k45l568d2mmcf6qgcsevzx","topoheight":-1}}'. A registered account returns a balance; an unregistered one returns an error. Do this before any mainnet NFA template ships and before app/forms.js is allowed to offer a non-zero artificerFee. Repeat it against the private testnet daemon, where I expect it to be unregistered.
- **[DERO contracts — funds stuck forever, and states that need a human (directive §5, §29, LIVENESS BY CONSTRUCTION)] Real Monero stagenet / Zcash testnet wrap and unwrap, to see the vault's stuck states with actual foreign value behind them** — why: tools/vaultsigner has no Monero or Zcash backend at all (it exits on -backend monero|zcash|solana), and tools/xmrswap's Monero side has only ever run against an in-process fake wallet. Nothing here can attest a real deposit or pay a real withdrawal. — how: Run monerod --stagenet and one monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet --wallet-dir per signer; implement the Monero backend behind vaultsigner's Backend interface (Height/Finalized/Deposits/Verify/Address/ValidDest/Release/VerifyRelease/Reserve/Health); Register on a simulator vault, have the signers Attest a real subaddress, send stagenet XMR to it, watch Mint, then Burn with a refund address that has never registered on DERO and confirm on the simulator that Veto and Reclaim both revert exactly as LIV-04 shows — with real coins now stranded in the federation wallet against a claim that can never be settled.
- **[DERO contracts — funds stuck forever, and states that need a human (directive §5, §29, LIVENESS BY CONSTRUCTION)] A UI pass over the NFA and wrap screens to see what the app tells a user before each of these calls** — why: Budget, and the shared UI lock — the tests/run.sh lock was already contended with parallel agents this session. I read app/nfas.js and app/wrap.js instead. — how: tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-liveness, then read the PNGs. Check specifically: the Start sheet's charity field (app/nfas.js:95-101), whose copy promises 'the contract returns the unit otherwise' — false for an unregistered address; whether an ended auction still offers a Buy action to non-owners; and whether the wrap screen's burn form ever exposes a refund address other than the connected wallet.
- **[The DERO↔XMR trustless swap as a state machine under failure (contracts/htlc.bas + tools/xmrswap + tools/simhtlc) — directive §5, §6, §24, §25, §44] Any Monero stagenet run of the companion's Monero backend (tools/xmrswap/monero.go). NOT PERFORMED — every gate decision, every confirmation count and every sweep in this repository's history has been made against tools/xmrswap/fakewallet.go, an in-process single-chain model whose scanning is synchronous. BL-11.** — why: No monerod and no monero-wallet-rpc exist in this environment, and tools/simhtlc refuses to run without -fake-monero (main.go:69-71). The specific behaviours never observed: digest auth; generate_from_keys with restore_height followed by an immediate sweep_all (the fake scans instantly, a real wallet refreshes asynchronously and will most likely answer -37 'not enough unlocked money', which swap.go's sweep() treats as terminal with no retry); get_transfers min_height exclusivity; the locked / unlock_time / confirmations fields; get_balance.unlocked_balance excluding burnt duplicates; whether --wallet-dir is required for open_wallet by name. — how: On two machines (or two wallet-rpc ports): monerod --stagenet, then monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet --wallet-dir <dir> --rpc-bind-port 38082 [--rpc-login u:p] for each party. Fund Alice's stagenet wallet. Install htlc.bas on the local DERO simulator (cd tools && go run ./simhtlc -fake-monero -prover "<cmd>" prints an scid, or install it by hand). Then run xmrswap bob -monero http://127.0.0.1:38082 -net stagenet -monero-name <wallet> … and xmrswap alice … against that scid with the lowered -t1-min/-t2-min the simulator permits. Drive happy, refund and punish. Record the get_transfers, get_balance and sweep_all transcripts and compare them field by field with monero_test.go's fixtures, which are reference-example shapes, not recorded answers.
- **[The DERO↔XMR trustless swap as a state machine under failure (contracts/htlc.bas + tools/xmrswap + tools/simhtlc) — directive §5, §6, §24, §25, §44] A Monero reorganisation deeper than the confirmation depth after the gate (review R3). NOT PERFORMED.** — why: Neither the fake wallet nor any harness here can reorganise a chain, and Monero has no consensus bound on reorganisation depth — the 20-confirmation default is the one number in this protocol that is policy rather than proof, and docs/XMR_SWAP.md §10 says so. This is the single assumption on which the maker's whole position rests after he releases k_b. — how: It cannot be tested; it can only be measured. BL-30: pull a year of Monero block data and report the deepest observed reorganisation and its distribution, then choose Confirmations from that number rather than from the reported 18-block anecdote. Until then the honest statement is that the maker's XMR leg is protected by a probability estimate, not by a proof.
- **[The DERO↔XMR trustless swap as a state machine under failure (contracts/htlc.bas + tools/xmrswap + tools/simhtlc) — directive §5, §6, §24, §25, §44] A DERO reorganisation inside STABLE_LIMIT = 8, which is what Invariant 9, claimCutoff and finding XMR-06 exist for. NOT PERFORMED.** — why: tools/xmrswap/fakes_test.go's fakeChain has no reorg hook: heights only advance and locks are never un-mined, so neither the crash fuzz nor any test in the package has ever exercised the R2 race it claims to defend against. — how: Add an Unmine(k) to fakeChain that pops the last k blocks and reverts any lock state changed in them, then write two tests: (a) Alice's Claim mined at t1-3, reorganised, re-mined at t1 and refused — assert Bob's companion returns from 'claimed' to 'gate_sent' and never sweeps; (b) the same with a Claim re-sent through exit()'s retry loop inside the [t1-17, t1-10) band canResend permits, which is the case XMR-06 says has no margin left.
- **[The DERO↔XMR trustless swap as a state machine under failure (contracts/htlc.bas + tools/xmrswap + tools/simhtlc) — directive §5, §6, §24, §25, §44] Measurement of the real storage gas of Refund and Punish, so that -fee's default of 3,000 atomic units can be justified rather than assumed. NOT PERFORMED.** — why: The DVM harness reports install gas (3,598 for htlc.bas, seen this session) but not per-call storage gas, and the daemon's estimator refuses height-gated calls because it runs the contract at height 0 — which is exactly why the fixed fee exists. simhtlc's refund and punish paths were not run this session (chain time; the happy path alone took 228 s of simulator blocks). — how: Extend tests/htlc_test.go to print the storage gas the harness charges for Claim, Refund and Punish (it already prints it for the install), then run tools/withlock.sh dvm bash tests/run.sh -run Htlc -v. Cross-check against the simulator with go run ./simhtlc -fake-monero -prover "<cmd>" -paths refund,punish -fee <candidate>, bisecting -fee until the call is refused for gas. Set the default from the measured number plus a stated margin.
- **[The DERO↔XMR trustless swap as a state machine under failure (contracts/htlc.bas + tools/xmrswap + tools/simhtlc) — directive §5, §6, §24, §25, §44] The proof system itself (research/crosschain/poc/proof-xmr). NOT AUDITED here.** — why: Out of this dimension and unaudited anywhere: a Bulletproofs-R1CS SHA-256 gadget plus a Chaum-Pedersen sigma, 1,601 bytes, with no second implementation (BL-23). The companion trusts the external binary's exit code; a substituted binary that always exits 0 defeats Invariants 3 and 4 silently. I ran it (the live simhtlc run used the real WSL build and both proofs verified) but I did not review its soundness. — how: A Go verifier (BL-23) so the companion does not depend on an external process's exit status, plus an independent review of the R1CS gadget and the sigma binding. Until then, state plainly that a soundness flaw in proof-xmr means one party's preimage may not be its Monero key share, and the other party's coins land under a key nobody can complete.
- **[Integer/precision and fuzzing (directive §17, §43)] Real Monero stagenet and Zcash testnet amount handling: that a piconero amount above 2^53 survives monero-wallet-rpc's JSON, that get_transfers' amount/confirmations/unlock_time decode exactly, and that a ZIP 317 fee is computed and paid correctly on a real claim and refund.** — why: tools/xmrswap's Monero side has only ever run against the in-process fake (tools/xmrswap/fakewallet.go; tools/simhtlc/main.go:69-71 refuses to run without -fake-monero), tools/vaultsigner has no Monero or Zcash backend (main.go exits with 'not implemented yet'), and tools/zecswap has no node client, watcher or broadcast. The nodes are not run here and the code to drive them does not exist. — how: Monero: run monerod --stagenet and monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet --wallet-dir <dir> --rpc-bind-port 38082; fund a stagenet wallet; run `xmrswap bob` and `xmrswap alice` with -monero http://127.0.0.1:38082 -net stagenet against the local DERO simulator htlc; capture the raw get_transfers and get_balance JSON and assert every amount field is decoded into a uint64 identical to the string in the wire response (the client already uses typed uint64 struct fields at tools/xmrswap/monero.go:405-411 and 437, so this is a confirmation, not a search). Zcash: run zebrad on testnet with RPC enabled, derive two P2PKH keys, `zecswap script -hash H -claim <tm-addr> -refund <tm-addr> -cltv <tip+96> -net testnet`, pay the printed t2 address, then build the claim (Tx{BranchID from getblockchaininfo consensus.chaintip, In with Value and ScriptPubKey filled, Out P2PKH}), SigDigest(0), Key.Sign, ClaimScriptSig, Serialize, sendrawtransaction; repeat with RefundScriptSig, LockTime=cltv, Sequence != 0xffffffff after height cltv. The transaction builder and broadcast glue for this does not exist and must be written first (BL-40b).
- **[Integer/precision and fuzzing (directive §17, §43)] A long Go fuzz (hours rather than 60 s) of ParseV5 seeded with the ZIP 244 corpus, and a Go fuzz target over reference/uint128.go itself.** — why: The directive budgets 60 s per fuzz target and I kept to it. The uint128 coverage is already a 612,167-triple differential against math/big, so a fuzz target adds little; ParseV5 would benefit from a longer run with a real corpus, which the 60 s run began to build (47 entries). — how: Seed testdata/fuzz/FuzzParseV5NeverPanics/ with the serialized transactions from tools/zecswap/testdata/zip_0244.json, then `cd tools && go test ./zecswap -fuzz FuzzParseV5NeverPanics -fuzztime 2h`. Commit whatever new corpus entries it finds. For uint128, add `FuzzMulDivMatchesBig(f *testing.F)` in tests/ calling ref.MulDiv/MulDivUp/Bps/BpsUp against their *Big counterparts and run `VEIL_FUZZ_N=... tests/run.sh -fuzz FuzzMulDiv -fuzztime 10m`.
- **[Integer/precision and fuzzing (directive §17, §43)] A UI pass rendering the Founders Pass screen against the simulator to photograph INT-01 in the page rather than in the formula.** — why: My wallet allocation for this pass is read-only, so I could not Deposit the ~42,950 DERO per registered unit needed to roll the accumulator on the live simulator vault, and the UI lock is shared with several other auditors running concurrently. — how: With a funded wallet: deploy a founders instance with a small maxsupply, Register one unit, then Deposit enough DERO to make acc + inc cross 2^64 (with hst = 1 that is 4294967296 atomic units per unit of acc, i.e. about 42,950 DERO), then `tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=... node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-int01` and read the NFTs tab PNG: the pending figure will read 0 (or half) while `paid` and `rpool` on the same card say otherwise.
- **[Integer/precision and fuzzing (directive §17, §43)] Driving vault.bas's mint-window accumulator (mw_<bucket>) to wrap.** — why: mw_ sums minted amounts within a 2000-block bucket; every Mint requires sup + amount <= cap <= 2^63-1, so reaching 2^64 in one bucket needs the supply to be burned and released and re-minted thousands of times inside 2000 blocks. I could not construct it, and a wrap there would relax a rate limit rather than move value. — how: In the harness, seal a vault with cap = 2^63-1 and winmax non-zero, then loop Mint / Burn / Released within one 2000-block bucket until mw_<bucket> + mw_<bucket-1> exceeds 2^64, asserting at each step that vault.bas line 59's window check still refuses a full window.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] A ring-size>2 TELA INDEX install, to confirm on chain that TELA-INDEX-1 stores owner == "anon" and that UpdateCode is then permanently refused — the only verifiable revocation path available for the app publisher key (SEC-K-03).** — why: I was assigned read-only wallets, so I could not send an install transaction; and telainstall hard-codes ring 2 (main.go:232, :249), so the shipped tool cannot do it as written. — how: On the local simulator: run `vendor/bin/tela-cli.exe` install-index against 127.0.0.1:20000 from a funded simulator wallet, answering ring size 16 at the prompt (or add a -ring flag to tools/telainstall and pass 16 to tela.Installer). Then `curl -s -X POST http://127.0.0.1:20000/json_rpc -d '{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":"1","method":"DERO.GetSC","params":{"scid":"<index scid>","variables":true}}'` and confirm the `owner` key is the literal string "anon"; then attempt UpdateCode from the installing wallet and confirm RETURN 1.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] Reproducing the mainnet wallet-RPC authentication failure end to end (SEC-K-08).** — why: It needs a mainnet daemon and a funded mainnet wallet, and the directive forbids touching mainnet. netwallet only enforces -rpc-login when -network mainnet. — how: Without funds, force the same code path on testnet: start a private testnet daemon, then `vendor/bin/netwallet.exe -network testnet -daemon 127.0.0.1:40402 -wallet .testnet/deployer.db -rpc-login u:p -rpc-bind 127.0.0.1:40406`, and run `cd tools && go run ./simdeploy -network testnet -w0 127.0.0.1:40406 -w2 127.0.0.1:40404 -daemon 127.0.0.1:40402`. Expect simrpc to fail decoding the HTTP 401 body ('Authorization Required'), because simrpc.go:41/:270 send no Authorization header. Repeat with treasurybot and vaultsigner pointed at the same port.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] Demonstrating the TELA port-recycling storage and XSWD-identity collision (SEC-K-16).** — why: It needs two distinct TELA sites served through the tela package on the same machine, and a browser session driven across a close/reopen cycle; the UI harness serves one bundle from tools/serve on a fixed port and never exercises tela.FindOpenPort. — how: Install two INDEXes on the simulator with different dURLs (`telainstall -durl dmz.tela -app dist/simulator` and a second trivial bundle with `-durl other.tela`). Serve DMZ first with `vendor/bin/tela-cli.exe serve <dmz index>` (it takes 8082), open it, connect the wallet, publish a trade so veil.trades is written; stop that server; serve the second site (it now takes 8082) and read localStorage from its page. Then from the second site set sessionStorage veil.endpointOk to a wss URL and `window.open('http://localhost:8082/index.html')` after re-serving DMZ on 8082, and observe whether app.connectNode uses the unconfirmed endpoint.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] Verifying that the commits pinned in deploy/vendor.lock are the commits upstream actually published (BL-206), and that the vendored derohe/tela/civilware trees are not a substituted clone.** — why: tools/verifyvendor only compares the local clone's HEAD and origin URL against the lock; it never contacts upstream. I spent the session's budget on the contract and key surfaces instead. — how: WebFetch https://github.com/deroproject/derohe/commit/e9df1205b6603c62f0651d0e18e5e77a2584b15e , https://github.com/civilware/derohe/commit/433c45590f6792117ff81a2eb3e45513bb856ff5 , https://github.com/civilware/tela/commit/aa892d2ff8d4deb9c519dd19c135e36ff9eac12b and https://github.com/DEROFDN/Engram/commit/12b6e61a592b9f69320e98e12f023cc0736f0dc6 , and confirm each exists on the named branch with the tree hash the local clone reports (`git -C vendor/<repo> rev-parse HEAD^{tree}`). Then add that fetch to verifyvendor behind a -online flag.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] Any real Monero stagenet or Zcash testnet key-handling test for the federation signer — whether the multisig/FROST key shares are ever written to disk, in what form, and with what permissions.** — why: tools/vaultsigner has no Monero or Zcash backend at all: main.go:470-476 exits with log.Fatalf('not implemented yet') for -backend monero|zcash|solana. The only backend is `sim`, a JSON file, which holds no keys. There is therefore no signer key material in this repository to audit. — how: Once the backends exist (docs/HANDOVER.md §5.3 items 1-7 for Monero, §5.4 items 1-5 for Zcash): run monerod --stagenet plus one monero-wallet-rpc per signer with a multisig wallet, point vaultsigner at it with -backend monero, complete a Register/Attest/deposit/Mint and a Burn/Paying/Release cycle, then audit the wallet-dir and the vaultsigner state file for key shares, their file modes, and whether they survive process exit.
- **[Secrets, keys, privileges, emergency controls (directive §27, §28, §29)] A UI pass confirming what the wrap, Settings and notice screens actually render for the claims in SEC-K-03, SEC-K-10 and SEC-K-13.** — why: The UI lock (tools/withlock.sh ui) and the DVM package were both being contended by other finder agents in this session — the tests package would not compile at the end of my run because of another agent's in-progress files (zzadv_market_test.go, zzsplitpay_test.go). I read the source and the previously rendered .sim/ui-gates-pass PNGs instead. — how: tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-secrets , then read .sim/ui-secrets/*.png with the Read tool and check: the Settings 'Security & privacy' list for the 'There is no operator' line and the CSP sentence; the wrap trust panel with a junk viewkey set on the simulator vault; and the build stamp in the rail foot against the on-chain `commit` of the simulator INDEX ece54602….
- **[price, quotes, liquidity, counterparty, front-running, griefing (directive §18, §19, §30, standing requirement 5)] A live sandwich / front-running run on the DERO simulator: open the trade sheet on a simulator market, leave it open across blocks, and have a second wallet move the pool in the interval so the stale min_out is hit exactly (ADV-PRICE-03).** — why: My assigned wallet access is read-only, so I cannot sign a swap, seed a pool or install a contract on the simulator. The evidence for ADV-PRICE-03 is therefore a code trace plus the vendored chain's serialization, not an executed exploit. — how: With two funded simulator wallets (e.g. 30003 and 30004) and a DERO-quoted pool holding at least 1e8 atomic units: (1) serve the app and open the trade sheet on that pool at http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html, type an amount, note the 'Minimum received' figure and do not sign; (2) from the second wallet, execute SwapDeroForAsset against the same pool with roughly 5% of rd, repeatedly, over ~10 blocks; (3) confirm the sheet's price line, 'You receive' and 'Minimum received' have not changed (that is the defect); (4) sign; (5) read the resulting transaction with DERO.GetTransaction and compare the amount received against the live pool price at the inclusion block. To show the mempool half, poll the daemon's transaction pool while a swap is unconfirmed and print its SCDATA and payload BurnValue — both are plaintext (vendor/derohe/transaction/transaction.go:87,92,117,475).
- **[price, quotes, liquidity, counterparty, front-running, griefing (directive §18, §19, §30, standing requirement 5)] A UI pass (tools/withlock.sh ui env PW_DIR=<playwright> node tools/uitest/ui.mjs http://127.0.0.1:8082/index.html .sim/ui-adv) rendering the trade sheet on a market quoted in an asset the deployment does not name, to photograph the false 'settles in wrapped <hex>' box and the plain green Verified badge (ADV-PRICE-05).** — why: No such market exists on the simulator, and creating one needs an install plus RegisterLaunch, i.e. a spending wallet I do not have. Rendering the existing simulator markets would not show the defect. — how: From a funded simulator wallet: (1) install tests' assetCode (or any 12-decimal launch) to get an SCID that is NOT in app/config.js vaults[]; (2) install app/templates/launch.bas with quote set to that SCID, treasury = the factory's tstr, fee = 50, pfee = 5000; (3) call factory RegisterLaunch with regfee (10000 atomic); (4) run the UI pass and open #token/<new scid> then the Buy sheet; (5) read the PNGs — expect the green 'Verified' badge, a 'This market settles in wrapped <6 hex>.' box asserting one-for-one federation backing, a 'Get wrapped <6 hex>' button routing to #wrap, and a Liquidity figure inflated by 10^7 with no thin warning.
- **[price, quotes, liquidity, counterparty, front-running, griefing (directive §18, §19, §30, standing requirement 5)] Exercising a halted vault against a live wrapped-quote market, to show that a pool/launch/NFA priced in wXMR keeps quoting and settling with no reading of the vault's state (ADV-PRICE-06).** — why: Halt(1) needs k signer votes (vault.bas:503-521) from the federation wallets; those are spending calls I cannot make read-only. — how: On the simulator XMR vault f23403b0…: (1) run tools/vaultsigner or drive Halt(1) directly from two of wallets 30003/30004/30005 within one 2000-block vote bucket until halted > tip; (2) confirm with DERO.GetSC on the vault that halted exceeds the current height; (3) open a wXMR-quoted market's trade sheet in the app WITHOUT first visiting the Wrap screen and read the PNG — expect no mention of the halt anywhere; (4) visit #wrap for that vault, return to the NFA page, and confirm nfas.js:165's warning appears only then, which is the session-order dependence the finding describes.
- **[price, quotes, liquidity, counterparty, front-running, griefing (directive §18, §19, §30, standing requirement 5)] Any Monero stagenet or Zcash testnet exercise of the swap/board/htlc counterparty flows.** — why: tools/vaultsigner has no Monero or Zcash backend (it exits on -backend monero|zcash), tools/xmrswap's Monero side has only ever run against the in-process fake wallet, and tools/zecswap has no watcher, no transaction builder and no broadcast. The operator does not run these nodes. Nothing in my dimension depended on it, but the board and escrow griefing findings are contract-level only for that reason. — how: For the ZEC leg: run zebrad on testnet with RPC enabled; derive two P2PKH keys; `zecswap script -hash H -claim <tm-addr> -refund <tm-addr> -cltv <tip+96> -net testnet`; fund the printed t2 address; build, sign and broadcast the claim and refund spends (the builder and sendrawtransaction glue do not exist in tools/zecswap and must be written first — see BL-40b). For the XMR leg: monerod --stagenet plus monero-wallet-rpc --stagenet --wallet-dir on both sides, then `xmrswap bob` / `xmrswap alice` against the local simulator htlc with -net stagenet, driving the happy, refund and punish exits and recording the get_transfers and sweep_all transcripts (BL-11).
- **[price, quotes, liquidity, counterparty, front-running, griefing (directive §18, §19, §30, standing requirement 5)] Measuring what a launch graduation actually costs to manipulate on a wrapped quote (the grad bound is quote-agnostic: launch.bas allows grad in [1e6, 1e15], which on a 12-decimal quote is 0.000001 to 1000 XMR).** — why: Needs an installed wXMR-quoted launch and a spending wallet. The arithmetic is stated in the fine list; I did not put a measured number on it. — how: Install launch.bas with quote = the XMR vault SCID and grad = 1000000 (the contract minimum, i.e. 1e-6 XMR); buy the whole curve from one wallet; confirm phase flips to 1 with rd = real ≈ 1e6 atomic and ra = Lq, and that the app then shows the market as a graduated AMM with a 'curve 100%' history. Compare against BL-104, which records the same class of mistake made honestly by a creator.
